sample 49: Okay, let's break down heterosis (also often called hybrid vigor). Here's a comprehensive explanation, covering the basics, the science behind it, examples, and some nuances.

**What is Heterosis? (The Simple Explanation)**

Heterosis is the improved or increased function of any biological quality in a hybrid offspring. It's the phenomenon where the offspring of two different parent lines (often inbred lines) exhibits superior traits compared to either parent line.
